season</summary><div class=\"reads-body\"><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl pir\">PIRATE</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">PIRATE: the Pirate capper's own pre-game over/under pick. The % next to it is the LIVE chance that exact pick still cashes given the current score, hits and inning (from past games in the same state; hits-aware when available) \u2014 it updates as the game goes. Green = likely, cyan = unlikely.</span></span> <b>Pirates Pick:</b> <b class=\"o\">over 7.5</b> &mdash; <b style=\"color:var(--tx)\">52.7%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> season \u00b7 n=916</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl alt\">ALT</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">ALT (the fade option): the best INDEPENDENT convergence on the side OPPOSITE the Pirate's pick \u2014 the non-Pirate source combination (or single) that took the other side today and whose past O/U calls, when those same sources agreed, won the most often this season (graded vs each game's own line). Shown ONLY when a real opposing convergence exists and clears the vig (~52.4%), so it's always a credible counter to the Pirate, never the same side. The % is the season hit rate (fair no-vig odds + sample beside it); 'thin sample' flags a light record. It's yellow-flagged 'beats Pirate's \u2026' when it tops the Pirate's own season rate on his side \u2014 a genuine fade signal. Ranked purely on win rate (a 60% pair outranks a 53% single). Note: beefai & beef are near-clones \u2014 a combo with both is one effective vote.</span></span> <b>CLAUDEAI:</b> <b class=\"u\">UNDER 8.5</b> &mdash; <b style=\"color:var(--tx)\">52.5%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> hit (-111 &middot; n=99)</span><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> &middot; vs Pirate's over 52.7%</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl pir\">PIR F5</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">PIR F5: the Pirate's first-five moneyline pick (the team he likes to lead after 5 innings). Reference only \u2014 F5_ML tested as no better than overall team strength, which the moneyline already prices.</span></span> <b>Pirate F5 (1st-5 ML):</b> <b>TB</b> <span class=\"muted nsmall\">\u2014 reference</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl season\">SEASON</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">SEASON: each team's share of its OWN runs scored in innings 1-5 this season (a descriptive tendency \u2014 front-loaded if high, back-loaded if low). Reference only: tested as a live-total signal and it did NOT beat conditioning on the current score, so it's shown to inform, not to bet.</span></span> <b>F5 Runs</b> <span class=\"muted nsmall\">(2026)</span>: TB <b>55.2%</b> &middot; DET <b>58.3%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> \u2014 league 55.8%</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl wx\">BALLPARK</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">BALLPARK: conditions at THIS park \u2014 temperature, humidity and the wind relative to the park itself (out toward centre carries, in from centre kills). 'stadium' is MLB's own observation, which only appears near first pitch; 'forecast' is modelled for the game hour. REFERENCE ONLY, and the measured reason is worth knowing: over 874 open-air games with a posted total, temperature swings run scoring hard (8.02 under 60F to 11.52 at 90F+) while wind barely moves it (+0.28 blowing out 8+) \u2014 the reverse of the folklore \u2014 and the market prices BOTH, so every band lands at ~50% against the game's own line. It tells you what KIND of game to expect, not which side to bet.</span></span> \ud83c\udf21\ufe0f <b>74&deg;F</b> (feels 71&deg;) &middot; \ud83d\udca7 51% hum &middot; \ud83d\udca8 12 mph out to CF &middot; <b class=\"o\">\u2197\ufe0f 12.0 mph carrying OUT</b> <span class=\"muted nsmall\">(stadium)</span></div></div></details></div><div class=\"g \" data-rank=\"3\" data-time=\"2026-08-25T01:40:00Z\" data-inning=\"100\" data-gk=\"823097\" data-gstate=\"pre\" data-sport=\"MLB\" data-mu=\"PHI@SEA\" data-alert=\"\" data-search=\"phi philadelphia phillies phils sea seattle mariners mlb baseball\"><div class=\"hd\"><button class=\"clpb\" title=\"Collapse / expand this game\">&#9662;</button><span style=\"display:inline-block;background:rgba(155,89,182,.18);color:#c8a2d8;border:1px solid #9b59b6;border-radius:4px;padding:0 6px;font-size:12px;margin-right:7px\">#3</span>PHI @ SEA <span class=\"st\">Scheduled &middot; 9:40 PM</span> <button class=\"gob\" data-gk=\"823097\" title=\"Re-price THIS game's odds now (everyone can \u2014 brief cooldown per game). season</summary><div class=\"reads-body\"><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl pir\">PIRATE</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">PIRATE: the Pirate capper's own pre-game over/under pick. The % next to it is the LIVE chance that exact pick still cashes given the current score, hits and inning (from past games in the same state; hits-aware when available) \u2014 it updates as the game goes. Green = likely, cyan = unlikely.</span></span> <b>Pirates Pick:</b> <b class=\"o\">over 6.5</b> &mdash; <b style=\"color:var(--tx)\">52.7%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> season \u00b7 n=916</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl con\">CON</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">CON (convergence confidence): a SAME-SIDE convergence \u2014 2+ sources that agree with the Pirate (and may include him, e.g. PIRATE+BLACKBEARD+BEEFAI) \u2014 whose past O/U calls, when those same sources agreed, won MORE often this season than the Pirate's own rate on this side, and clears the vig. Shown only when such a stronger same-side pattern exists, so it's pure added confidence behind his pick (never the opposite side). The % is the season hit rate (fair no-vig odds + sample beside it); 'thin sample' flags a light record. Ranked by win rate. Note: beefai & beef are near-clones \u2014 a combo with both is one effective vote.</span></span> <b>BEEFAI+BLACKBEARD+PIRATE:</b> <b class=\"o\">OVER 6.5</b> &mdash; <b style=\"color:var(--tx)\">55.8%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> hit (-126 &middot; n=129) &middot; confirms Pirate</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl alt\">ALT</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">ALT (the fade option): the best INDEPENDENT convergence on the side OPPOSITE the Pirate's pick \u2014 the non-Pirate source combination (or single) that took the other side today and whose past O/U calls, when those same sources agreed, won the most often this season (graded vs each game's own line). Shown ONLY when a real opposing convergence exists and clears the vig (~52.4%), so it's always a credible counter to the Pirate, never the same side. The % is the season hit rate (fair no-vig odds + sample beside it); 'thin sample' flags a light record. It's yellow-flagged 'beats Pirate's \u2026' when it tops the Pirate's own season rate on his side \u2014 a genuine fade signal. Ranked purely on win rate (a 60% pair outranks a 53% single). Note: beefai & beef are near-clones \u2014 a combo with both is one effective vote.</span></span> <b>CLAUDEAI:</b> <b class=\"u\">UNDER 7.5</b> &mdash; <b style=\"color:var(--tx)\">52.5%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> hit (-111 &middot; n=99)</span><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> &middot; vs Pirate's over 52.7%</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl pir\">PIR F5</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">PIR F5: the Pirate's first-five moneyline pick (the team he likes to lead after 5 innings). Reference only \u2014 F5_ML tested as no better than overall team strength, which the moneyline already prices.</span></span> <b>Pirate F5 (1st-5 ML):</b> <b>PHI</b> <span class=\"muted nsmall\">\u2014 reference</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl season\">SEASON</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">SEASON: each team's share of its OWN runs scored in innings 1-5 this season (a descriptive tendency \u2014 front-loaded if high, back-loaded if low). Reference only: tested as a live-total signal and it did NOT beat conditioning on the current score, so it's shown to inform, not to bet.</span></span> <b>F5 Runs</b> <span class=\"muted nsmall\">(2026)</span>: PHI <b>53.9%</b> &middot; SEA <b>56.5%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> \u2014 league 55.8%</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl wx\">BALLPARK</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">BALLPARK: conditions at THIS park \u2014 temperature, humidity and the wind relative to the park itself (out toward centre carries, in from centre kills). 'stadium' is MLB's own observation, which only appears near first pitch; 'forecast' is modelled for the game hour. REFERENCE ONLY, and the measured reason is worth knowing: over 874 open-air games with a posted total, temperature swings run scoring hard (8.02 under 60F to 11.52 at 90F+) while wind barely moves it (+0.28 blowing out 8+) \u2014 the reverse of the folklore \u2014 and the market prices BOTH, so every band lands at ~50% against the game's own line. It tells you what KIND of game to expect, not which side to bet.</span></span> \ud83c\udf21\ufe0f <b>75&deg;F</b> (feels 75&deg;) &middot; \ud83d\udca7 59% hum &middot; \ud83d\udca8 10 mph in from LF &middot; <b class=\"u\">\u2198\ufe0f 7.1 mph knocking DOWN</b> <span class=\"muted nsmall\">(stadium)</span></div></div></details></div><div class=\"g \" data-rank=\"4\" data-time=\"2026-08-24T22:40:00Z\" data-inning=\"100\" data-gk=\"823828\" data-gstate=\"pre\" data-sport=\"MLB\" data-mu=\"BOS@MIA\" data-alert=\"\" data-search=\"bos boston red sox bosox mia miami marlins fish mlb baseball\"><div class=\"hd\"><button class=\"clpb\" title=\"Collapse / expand this game\">&#9662;</button><span style=\"display:inline-block;background:rgba(155,89,182,.18);color:#c8a2d8;border:1px solid #9b59b6;border-radius:4px;padding:0 6px;font-size:12px;margin-right:7px\">#4</span>BOS @ MIA <span class=\"st\">Scheduled &middot; 6:40 PM</span> <button class=\"gob\" data-gk=\"823828\" title=\"Re-price THIS game's odds now (everyone can \u2014 brief cooldown per game). season</summary><div class=\"reads-body\"><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl pir\">PIRATE</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">PIRATE: the Pirate capper's own pre-game over/under pick. The % next to it is the LIVE chance that exact pick still cashes given the current score, hits and inning (from past games in the same state; hits-aware when available) \u2014 it updates as the game goes. Green = likely, cyan = unlikely.</span></span> <b>Pirates Pick:</b> <b class=\"o\">over 7</b> &mdash; <b style=\"color:var(--tx)\">52.7%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> season \u00b7 n=916</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl con\">CON</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">CON (convergence confidence): a SAME-SIDE convergence \u2014 2+ sources that agree with the Pirate (and may include him, e.g. PIRATE+BLACKBEARD+BEEFAI) \u2014 whose past O/U calls, when those same sources agreed, won MORE often this season than the Pirate's own rate on this side, and clears the vig. Shown only when such a stronger same-side pattern exists, so it's pure added confidence behind his pick (never the opposite side). The % is the season hit rate (fair no-vig odds + sample beside it); 'thin sample' flags a light record. Ranked by win rate. Note: beefai & beef are near-clones \u2014 a combo with both is one effective vote.</span></span> <b>BEEFAI+BLACKBEARD+PIRATE:</b> <b class=\"o\">OVER 7</b> &mdash; <b style=\"color:var(--tx)\">55.8%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> hit (-126 &middot; n=129) &middot; confirms Pirate</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl alt\">ALT</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">ALT (the fade option): the best INDEPENDENT convergence on the side OPPOSITE the Pirate's pick \u2014 the non-Pirate source combination (or single) that took the other side today and whose past O/U calls, when those same sources agreed, won the most often this season (graded vs each game's own line). Shown ONLY when a real opposing convergence exists and clears the vig (~52.4%), so it's always a credible counter to the Pirate, never the same side. The % is the season hit rate (fair no-vig odds + sample beside it); 'thin sample' flags a light record. It's yellow-flagged 'beats Pirate's \u2026' when it tops the Pirate's own season rate on his side \u2014 a genuine fade signal. Ranked purely on win rate (a 60% pair outranks a 53% single). Note: beefai & beef are near-clones \u2014 a combo with both is one effective vote.</span></span> <b>CLAUDEAI:</b> <b class=\"u\">UNDER 8</b> &mdash; <b style=\"color:var(--tx)\">52.5%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> hit (-111 &middot; n=99)</span><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> &middot; vs Pirate's over 52.7%</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl season\">SEASON</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">SEASON: each team's share of its OWN runs scored in innings 1-5 this season (a descriptive tendency \u2014 front-loaded if high, back-loaded if low). Reference only: tested as a live-total signal and it did NOT beat conditioning on the current score, so it's shown to inform, not to bet.</span></span> <b>F5 Runs</b> <span class=\"muted nsmall\">(2026)</span>: BOS <b>55.7%</b> &middot; MIA <b>57.6%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> \u2014 league 55.8%</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl wx\">BALLPARK</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">BALLPARK: conditions at THIS park \u2014 temperature, humidity and the wind relative to the park itself (out toward centre carries, in from centre kills). 'stadium' is MLB's own observation, which only appears near first pitch; 'forecast' is modelled for the game hour. REFERENCE ONLY, and the measured reason is worth knowing: over 874 open-air games with a posted total, temperature swings run scoring hard (8.02 under 60F to 11.52 at 90F+) while wind barely moves it (+0.28 blowing out 8+) \u2014 the reverse of the folklore \u2014 and the market prices BOTH, so every band lands at ~50% against the game's own line. season</summary><div class=\"reads-body\"><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl pir\">PIRATE</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">PIRATE: the Pirate capper's own pre-game over/under pick. Green = likely, cyan = unlikely.</span></span> <b>Pirates Pick:</b> <b class=\"o\">over 7</b> &mdash; <b style=\"color:var(--tx)\">52.7%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> season \u00b7 n=916</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl alt\">ALT</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">ALT (the fade option): the best INDEPENDENT convergence on the side OPPOSITE the Pirate's pick \u2014 the non-Pirate source combination (or single) that took the other side today and whose past O/U calls, when those same sources agreed, won the most often this season (graded vs each game's own line). Shown ONLY when a real opposing convergence exists and clears the vig (~52.4%), so it's always a credible counter to the Pirate, never the same side. The % is the season hit rate (fair no-vig odds + sample beside it); 'thin sample' flags a light record. It's yellow-flagged 'beats Pirate's \u2026' when it tops the Pirate's own season rate on his side \u2014 a genuine fade signal. Ranked purely on win rate (a 60% pair outranks a 53% single). Note: beefai & beef are near-clones \u2014 a combo with both is one effective vote.</span></span> <b>CLAUDEAI:</b> <b class=\"u\">UNDER 7.5</b> &mdash; <b style=\"color:var(--tx)\">52.5%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> hit (-111 &middot; n=99)</span><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> &middot; vs Pirate's over 52.7%</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl season\">SEASON</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">SEASON: each team's share of its OWN runs scored in innings 1-5 this season (a descriptive tendency \u2014 front-loaded if high, back-loaded if low). Reference only: tested as a live-total signal and it did NOT beat conditioning on the current score, so it's shown to inform, not to bet.</span></span> <b>F5 Runs</b> <span class=\"muted nsmall\">(2026)</span>: CIN <b>59.8%</b> &middot; SF <b>55.3%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> \u2014 league 55.8%</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl wx\">BALLPARK</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">BALLPARK: conditions at THIS park \u2014 temperature, humidity and the wind relative to the park itself (out toward centre carries, in from centre kills). 'stadium' is MLB's own observation, which only appears near first pitch; 'forecast' is modelled for the game hour. season</summary><div class=\"reads-body\"><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl pir\">PIRATE</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">PIRATE: the Pirate capper's own pre-game over/under pick. The % next to it is the LIVE chance that exact pick still cashes given the current score, hits and inning (from past games in the same state; hits-aware when available) \u2014 it updates as the game goes. Green = likely, cyan = unlikely.</span></span> <b>Pirates Pick:</b> <b class=\"o\">over 8.5</b> &mdash; <b style=\"color:var(--tx)\">52.7%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> season \u00b7 n=916</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl con\">CON</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">CON (convergence confidence): a SAME-SIDE convergence \u2014 2+ sources that agree with the Pirate (and may include him, e.g. PIRATE+BLACKBEARD+BEEFAI) \u2014 whose past O/U calls, when those same sources agreed, won MORE often this season than the Pirate's own rate on this side, and clears the vig. Shown only when such a stronger same-side pattern exists, so it's pure added confidence behind his pick (never the opposite side). The % is the season hit rate (fair no-vig odds + sample beside it); 'thin sample' flags a light record. Ranked by win rate. Note: beefai & beef are near-clones \u2014 a combo with both is one effective vote.</span></span> <b>BEEFAI+CLAUDEAI+PIRATE:</b> <b class=\"o\">OVER 8.5</b> &mdash; <b style=\"color:var(--tx)\">56.0%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> hit (-127 &middot; n=25) &middot; confirms Pirate</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl pir\">PIR F5</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">PIR F5: the Pirate's first-five moneyline pick (the team he likes to lead after 5 innings). Reference only \u2014 F5_ML tested as no better than overall team strength, which the moneyline already prices.</span></span> <b>Pirate F5 (1st-5 ML):</b> <b>CHC</b> <span class=\"muted nsmall\">\u2014 reference</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl season\">SEASON</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">SEASON: each team's share of its OWN runs scored in innings 1-5 this season (a descriptive tendency \u2014 front-loaded if high, back-loaded if low). Reference only: tested as a live-total signal and it did NOT beat conditioning on the current score, so it's shown to inform, not to bet.</span></span> <b>F5 Runs</b> <span class=\"muted nsmall\">(2026)</span>: CHC <b>52.0%</b> &middot; ARI <b>53.5%</b><span class=\"muted nsmall\"> \u2014 league 55.8%</span></div><div class=\"ln\"><span class=\"lbl wx\">BALLPARK</span><span class=\"help\">?<span class=\"tip\">BALLPARK: conditions at THIS park \u2014 temperature, humidity and the wind relative to the park itself (out toward centre carries, in from centre kills). 'stadium' is MLB's own observation, which only appears near first pitch; 'forecast' is modelled for the game hour. REFERENCE ONLY, and the measured reason is worth knowing: over 874 open-air games with a posted total, temperature swings run scoring hard (8.02 under 60F to 11.52 at 90F+) while wind barely moves it (+0.28 blowing out 8+) \u2014 the reverse of the folklore \u2014 and the market prices BOTH, so every band lands at ~50% against the game's own line.
